- **stuck-detection**: Detect agent loops (repeated topics or repeated tool failures) and emit a self-reflection nudge. Use as an orchestrator guard against token-burning loops. Do NOT use for semantic similarity checks or multi-turn intent tracking; use a richer evaluation tool for those. - **stuck-detection**: Use the moment the same error, test failure, or rejection happens TWICE in a row, when you notice retry-with-minor-variation behavior, when elapsed effort is 2x your prediction, or when you're about to try the same approach "one more time but harder". Encodes the stop/strategy-switch/escalate ladder. Trigger proactively — by the third identical failure you are burning context and user trust. ([GhostlyGawd/recursive-harness](https://github.com/GhostlyGawd/recursive-harness/tree/HEAD/skills/stuck-detection) · ★3)
- **stuck-detector**: Use when the user says "stuck", "I'm stuck", "this isn't working", "tool keeps failing", "give up on this", "we're going in circles", "/stuck", or when the agent itself notices it has hit N consecutive tool errors in a session window. Generates a handoff summary so progress can resume in a fresh session, escalate to a stronger model, or pause for user input. Stops the doom-loop of blind retries. ([danmestas/wardrobe](https://github.com/danmestas/wardrobe/tree/HEAD/skills/stuck-detector) · ★4)
- **stuck-detector**: Notices when you are looping, stops, names the pattern and forces a different kind of action. Trigger the moment a tripwire fires mid-task (third attempt at the same idea, same command failing twice, a fix that breaks an earlier fix), or when the user says "you're going in circles", "you already tried that", "this isn't working" or "step back".
